=== SECTION 1: Burn mechanics ===

  PASS 2. Burned attacker special damage is NOT reduced
  PASS 3. Guts ability negates burn physical damage penalty

  PASS 7. Rage Powder sets redirection and "drawn to" appears when a non-Grass attacker is redirected
  PASS 8. Rage Powder: Grass-type attacker (Whimsicott) bypasses redirection
  PASS 9. Stalwart ability bypasses Follow Me / Rage Powder redirection

=== SECTION 4: Wide Guard / Quick Guard ===

  PASS 10. Wide Guard blocks a spread move (Rock Slide) for the whole team
  PASS 11. Quick Guard blocks a +1 priority move (Fake Out) for the team

  PASS 12. Spiky Shield deals chip damage to a contact-move attacker
  PASS 13. Baneful Bunker poisons a contact-move attacker

  PASS 14. U-turn deals damage and logs a switchout
  PASS 15. Parting Shot is used and logs the move

=== SECTION 7: Edge cases and interaction boundaries ===
Expected: type immunities block status, Frozen prevents action, Ice-type immune to freeze, Feint pierces Quick Guard, Wide Guard does not block single-target moves.
  PASS 16. Fire-type mon cannot be burned by Will-O-Wisp in live battle
  PASS 17. Frozen status prevents the afflicted mon from acting that turn
  PASS 18. Ice-type mon is immune to being frozen (canInflictStatus)
  PASS 19. Feint (priority +2) bypasses Quick Guard and deals damage
  PASS 20. Wide Guard does NOT block single-target moves ΓÇö only spread

RUN t9j12_lead_validation.js

=== T9j.12 lead-impact validation ===
Matchup: TR Counter Squad vs Mega Altaria
Sample: N=200 per cell, format=doubles

Player 6: [Incineroar, Arcanine, Garchomp, Whimsicott, Rotom-Wash, Dragapult]
Opp 6:    [Typhlosion-Hisui, Altaria-Mega, Whimsicott, Rotom-Wash, Sableye, Sinistcha]

  A original order                   bring [Incineroar, Arcanine, Garchomp, Whimsicott]                            W/L/D =  88/112/  0   wr = 44.0%   95% CI [37.3%, 50.9%]
  B swap LEADS 0<->1 (same 4)        bring [Arcanine, Incineroar, Garchomp, Whimsicott]                            W/L/D =   0/200/  0   wr = 0.0%   95% CI [0.0%, 1.9%]
  D swap BENCH 2<->3 (same 4)        bring [Incineroar, Arcanine, Whimsicott, Garchomp]                            W/L/D =  88/112/  0   wr = 44.0%   95% CI [37.3%, 50.9%]
  C different 4-of-6 (last 4)        bring [Garchomp, Whimsicott, Rotom-Wash, Dragapult]                           W/L/D = 198/  2/  0   wr = 99.0%   95% CI [96.4%, 99.7%]

Interpretation:
  A vs B (swap leads):        CI overlap = NO  - |dwr| = 44.0%
  A vs D (swap bench):        CI overlap = YES - |dwr| = 0.0%
  A vs C (different bring):   CI overlap = NO  - |dwr| = 55.0%
